parse	header	enforce	\-
= enforce/header self=- pre= []
parse	header	enforce	;
= enforce/header self=- pre=; []
parse	header	enforce	 ; ; 
= enforce/header self=- pre= ; ;  []
parse	header	enforce	default-src 'self'
= enforce/header self=- pre=default-src 'self' [default-src(DefaultSourceDirective) 'self']
parse	header	enforce	DEFAULT-SRC 'SELF' https://A.test
= enforce/header self=- pre=DEFAULT-SRC 'SELF' https://A.test [default-src(DefaultSourceDirective) 'SELF' https://A.test]
parse	header	enforce	script-src https://a.test; IMG-src *
= enforce/header self=- pre=script-src https://a.test; IMG-src * [script-src(ScriptSourceDirective) https://a.test ; img-src(ImageSourceDirective) *]
parse	header	enforce	script-src a; script-src b
= enforce/header self=- pre=script-src a; script-src b [script-src(ScriptSourceDirective) a]
parse	header	enforce	script-src a;script-src b
= enforce/header self=- pre=script-src a;script-src b [script-src(ScriptSourceDirective) a]
parse	header	enforce	  style-src\t'unsafe-inline'  \n  ;font-src data:
= enforce/header self=- pre=  style-src\t'unsafe-inline'  \n  ;font-src data: [style-src(StyleSourceDirective) 'unsafe-inline' ; font-src(FontSourceDirective) data:]
parse	header	enforce	unknown-directive value1  value2
= enforce/header self=- pre=unknown-directive value1  value2 [unknown-directive(Directive) value1 value2]
parse	header	enforce	default-src 'none'; report-uri /csp-report https://r.test/x; report-to group
= enforce/header self=- pre=default-src 'none'; report-uri /csp-report https://r.test/x; report-to group [default-src(DefaultSourceDirective) 'none' ; report-uri(ReportUriDirective) /csp-report https://r.test/x ; report-to(ReportToDirective) group]
parse	header	enforce	base-uri 'self'; child-src c; connect-src c; default-src d; font-src f; form-action fa; frame-ancestors 'none'; frame-src fs; img-src i; manifest-src m; media-src me; object-src o; report-to rt; report-uri ru; require-trusted-types-for 'script'; sandbox allow-scripts; script-src-attr sa; script-src s; script-src-elem se; style-src-attr sta; style-src st; style-src-elem ste; trusted-types tt; webrtc 'allow'; worker-src w
= enforce/header self=- pre=base-uri 'self'; child-src c; connect-src c; default-src d; font-src f; form-action fa; frame-ancestors 'none'; frame-src fs; img-src i; manifest-src m; media-src me; object-src o; report-to rt; report-uri ru; require-trusted-types-for 'script'; sandbox allow-scripts; script-src-attr sa; script-src s; script-src-elem se; style-src-attr sta; style-src st; style-src-elem ste; trusted-types tt; webrtc 'allow'; worker-src w [base-uri(BaseUriDirective) 'self' ; child-src(ChildSourceDirective) c ; connect-src(ConnectSourceDirective) c ; default-src(DefaultSourceDirective) d ; font-src(FontSourceDirective) f ; form-action(FormActionDirective) fa ; frame-ancestors(FrameAncestorsDirective) 'none' ; frame-src(FrameSourceDirective) fs ; img-src(ImageSourceDirective) i ; manifest-src(ManifestSourceDirective) m ; media-src(MediaSourceDirective) me ; object-src(ObjectSourceDirective) o ; report-to(ReportToDirective) rt ; report-uri(ReportUriDirective) ru ; require-trusted-types-for(RequireTrustedTypesForDirective) 'script' ; sandbox(SandboxDirective) allow-scripts ; script-src-attr(ScriptSourceAttributeDirective) sa ; script-src(ScriptSourceDirective) s ; script-src-elem(ScriptSourceElementDirective) se ; style-src-attr(StyleSourceAttributeDirective) sta ; style-src(StyleSourceDirective) st ; style-src-elem(StyleSourceElementDirective) ste ; trusted-types(Directive) tt ; webrtc(WebRTCDirective) 'allow' ; worker-src(WorkerSourceDirective) w]
